# PacEdu English Subject Box — Class 2 Printable Worksheets (Days 1–115)

**Status:** ORIGINAL PACEDU WORKSHEET DRAFT — `PENDING_TEACHER_REVIEW` — `PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION`. This is not a Ministry-issued worksheet pack.

## Printing and school calendar safeguards
- Each learning day has four separate versions: Copper, Bronze, Silver and Gold. Print only the learner's current pathway.
- School-local date, school timezone and approved calendar must be supplied by the viewer. Weekends, holidays and term breaks do not automatically advance the learning day.
- The official outcome/indicator and exact authorized source edition/page remain pending until verified against the school's current Fiji Ministry-authorized document.
- Accept appropriate speech, sign, AAC, pointing, gesture, drawing, dictation or writing. Do not force speech or mark accent/disability-related speech differences as errors.

**Learner:** ____________________ **School:** ____________________ **Teacher:** ____________________

## Day 1 — Listen and speak — Copper Worksheet

**Shared learning intention:** Introduce self; ask and answer simple questions; recognise names and familiar words.

**PacEdu working achievement indicator:** Teacher records the observable response for this day's target.

**Your task:** Complete the activity modelled by your teacher using the current taught sequence.

**Worked example:** Teacher models one example, explains each step, and completes a second example with the learner.

**Copper pathway:** Complete the same objective with the level of modelling and support appropriate to this pathway.

**Teacher prompt:** Show or explain how you know.

**My response / working:**

____________________________________________________________________

____________________________________________________________________

____________________________________________________________________

**Evidence to record:** Record the learner's actual response, communication mode and support used.

**Quick check:** Can the learner show the target skill in one new example? Record independently / with prompt / with model / not yet observed.

**Official indicator mapping:** PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION — add exact authorized wording, source edition and page only after school-source verification.

## Release gate
A qualified Fiji English teacher must review all 115 days for level suitability, progression, accessibility, local relevance and alignment with the school's current authorized syllabus. Physical Android Chrome print preview must confirm one selected worksheet prints on one page. Do not release to learners until review and source verification are complete.
